### (a) LIVE 4c5f245f direct permits (objects)  [engine=kuzu-cypher]

### (a) LIVE 4c5f245f grant-derived reach (identity path)  [engine=kuzu-cypher]
CYPHER:

              -[:member_of*0..1]->(g:Identity)-[gr:grants_to]->(o:DatabricksObject)
        RETURN u.id AS principal, g.id AS via_group, o.obj_id AS asset,
               gr.privilege AS privilege, gr.count AS rows
        ORDER BY gr.count DESC
OUTPUT:
principal | via_group | asset | privilege | rows

### (b) orphan findings (zero tested paths)  [engine=kuzu-cypher]
CYPHER:
MATCH (f:Finding)
    WHERE NOT EXISTS { MATCH (:DatabricksObject)-[:referenced_by_obj]->(f) }
      AND NOT EXISTS { MATCH (:Host)-[:referenced_by_host]->(f) }
      AND NOT EXISTS { MATCH (:S3Bucket)-[:referenced_by_bucket]->(f) }
      AND NOT EXISTS { MATCH (:Credential)-[:referenced_by_cred]->(f) }
    RETURN f.vul_id AS finding, f.severity AS severity, f.status AS status,
           f.title AS title
OUTPUT:
finding | severity | status | title
vul_092d55f5c001 | LOW | CLOSED | Concur finding (lane CLOSED)
OTX-N1 | INFO | REPORT-ONLY | OTX pulse clone corpus — brand mention, ZERO pivot value
OTX-N2 | INFO | REPORT-ONLY | OTX Pegasus research tag — corpus mention, ZERO pivot value
OTX-N3 | INFO | REPORT-ONLY | OTX whitelist pulse — benign, ZERO pivot value

### (c) TiDB infra transitive closure (1..3 hops)  [engine=kuzu-cypher]
CYPHER:
MATCH (root:Host {kind:'tidb-infra'})-[r:connects_to*1..3]->(h:Host)
    RETURN root.name AS root, h.name AS reachable, length(r) AS hops
    ORDER BY root.name, hops, h.name

### (c) TiDB infra roots census  [engine=kuzu-cypher]
CYPHER:
MATCH (h:Host {kind:'tidb-infra'}) RETURN count(h) AS tidb_host_nodes
OUTPUT:

### (d) NEW endpoints -> LIVE credential (via host adjacency 0..2)  [engine=kuzu-cypher]
CYPHER:
MATCH (h:Host)-[:connects_to*0..2]->(x:Host)<-[:validated_against]-(c:Credential {verdict:'LIVE'})
    WHERE h.new_lane IN ['31','35','38']
    RETURN DISTINCT h.name AS new_endpoint, h.new_lane AS lane,
           c.sha8 AS live_cred, x.name AS via_host
    ORDER BY lane, new_endpoint
OUTPUT:
new_endpoint | lane | live_cred | via_host
bolt-common.cloud.databricks.com | 31 | 2e1ce409 | bolt-common.cloud.databricks.com
bolt-common.cloud.databricks.com | 31 | 4c5f245f | bolt-common.cloud.databricks.com

### (d) NEW endpoints -> Databricks object (via host adjacency 0..2)  [engine=kuzu-cypher]
CYPHER:
MATCH (h:Host)-[:connects_to*0..2]->(x:Host)<-[:reachable_via_obj]-(o:DatabricksObject)
    WHERE h.new_lane IN ['31','35','38']
    RETURN DISTINCT h.name AS new_endpoint, h.new_lane AS lane, o.obj_id AS object
    ORDER BY lane, new_endpoint
OUTPUT:
new_endpoint | lane | object
bolt-common.cloud.databricks.com | 31 | main.sel_geo.geoextraction_configs
bolt-common.cloud.databricks.com | 31 | link:5d9d0299
bolt-common.cloud.databricks.com | 31 | main.ng_incentives.campaign_operations_market_month_budget_targets

    RETURN count(h) AS new_endpoint_count

### (e) SINGLE-IDENTITY CHAIN TEST: LIVE creds holding BOTH legs  [engine=kuzu-cypher]
CYPHER:
MATCH (c:Credential {verdict:'LIVE'})
    WITH c,
         EXISTS { MATCH (c)-[:permits_obj]->(:DatabricksObject {obj_id:'main.sel_geo.geoextraction_configs'}) } AS leg1,
         EXISTS { MATCH (c)-[:permits_obj]->(:DatabricksObject {obj_id:'link:5d9d0299'}) } AS leg2
    OPTIONAL MATCH (c)-[:resolves_to]->(u:Identity)
    RETURN c.sha8 AS live_cred, u.id AS identity, leg1, leg2
OUTPUT:
live_cred | identity | leg1 | leg2

09aeaf62 |  | False | False
